974 entries, comment-sicko to competitive-analyst. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **comment-sicko**: Comment review of one repo for one wave of a ticket, before or beside the wave review. Flags every comment the diff adds that the no-comments rule (tix:practices) doesn't keep, and the code each surprise-explaining comment should be reshaped into, as should-fix findings in tickets/&lt;source&gt;/&lt;ID&gt;/reviews/wave-N-&lt;slug&gt;-comments.md. Read-only on code. Spawned by the PO (/tix:work-ticket) at the lead's request. ([zero4573/tix](https://github.com/zero4573/tix/blob/HEAD/assets/plugin/agents/comment-sicko.md))
- **comment-sicko-agent**: A deranged comment-hater that savors deletion and condemns workaround code.
